\section{Noninterference}



 As explained in the \coqdoclibrary{Noninterference} chapter, data
    confidentiality is most often expressed formally as a property
    called \textit{noninterference}.


    To formalize this for Imp programs, we divide the variables as
    either public or secret by assuming a total map \coqdocvar{P} : \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.pub vars}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_vars}}
    between variables and Boolean labels: 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Definition} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.label}{label}{\coqdocdefinition{label}} := \coqexternalref{bool}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Datatypes}{\coqdocinductive{bool}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Definition} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.public}{public}{\coqdocdefinition{public}} : \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.label}{\coqdocdefinition{label}} := \coqref{SECF.Imp.:::'true'}{\coqdocnotation{true}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Definition} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.secret}{secret}{\coqdocdefinition{secret}} : \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.label}{\coqdocdefinition{label}} := \coqref{SECF.Imp.:::'false'}{\coqdocnotation{false}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Definition} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.pub vars}{pub\_vars}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_vars}} := \coqref{SECF.Maps.total map}{\coqdocdefinition{total\_map}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.label}{\coqdocdefinition{label}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
A noninterference attacker can only observe the final values of public
    variables, not of secret ones. We formalize this as a notion of
    \textit{publicly equivalent states} that agree on the values of all
    public variables, which are thus indistinguishable to an attacker: 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Definition} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.pub equiv}{pub\_equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}} (\coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.P:1}{P}{\coqdocbinder{P}} : \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub vars}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_vars}}) \{\coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.X:2}{X}{\coqdocbinder{X}}:\coqdockw{Type}\} (\coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:3}{s1}{\coqdocbinder{s1}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:4}{s2}{\coqdocbinder{s2}} : \coqref{SECF.Maps.total map}{\coqdocdefinition{total\_map}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.X:2}{\coqdocvariable{X}}) :=\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqdockw{\ensuremath{\forall}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.x:5}{x}{\coqdocbinder{x}}:\coqdocinductive{string}, \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.P:1}{\coqdocvariable{P}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.x:5}{\coqdocvariable{x}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '=' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{=}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.public}{\coqdocdefinition{public}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '->' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\rightarrow}}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:3}{\coqdocvariable{s1}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.x:5}{\coqdocvariable{x}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '=' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{=}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:4}{\coqdocvariable{s2}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.x:5}{\coqdocvariable{x}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
For some total map \coqdocvar{P} from variables to Boolean labels,
    \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.pub equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}} \coqdocvar{P} is an equivalence relation on states, so reflexive,
    symmetric, and transitive. 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Lemma} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.pub equiv refl}{pub\_equiv\_refl}{\coqdoclemma{pub\_equiv\_refl}} : \coqdockw{\ensuremath{\forall}} \{\coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.X:6}{X}{\coqdocbinder{X}}:\coqdockw{Type}\} (\coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.P:7}{P}{\coqdocbinder{P}} : \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub vars}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_vars}}) (\coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s:8}{s}{\coqdocbinder{s}} : \coqref{SECF.Maps.total map}{\coqdocdefinition{total\_map}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.X:6}{\coqdocvariable{X}}),\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.P:7}{\coqdocvariable{P}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s:8}{\coqdocvariable{s}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s:8}{\coqdocvariable{s}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Proof}. \coqdoctac{intros} \coqdocvar{X} \coqdocvar{P} \coqdocvar{s} \coqdocvar{x} \coqdocvar{Hx}. \coqdoctac{reflexivity}. \coqdockw{Qed}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Lemma} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.pub equiv sym}{pub\_equiv\_sym}{\coqdoclemma{pub\_equiv\_sym}} : \coqdockw{\ensuremath{\forall}} \{\coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.X:9}{X}{\coqdocbinder{X}}:\coqdockw{Type}\} (\coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.P:10}{P}{\coqdocbinder{P}} : \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub vars}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_vars}}) (\coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:11}{s1}{\coqdocbinder{s1}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:12}{s2}{\coqdocbinder{s2}} : \coqref{SECF.Maps.total map}{\coqdocdefinition{total\_map}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.X:9}{\coqdocvariable{X}}),\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.P:10}{\coqdocvariable{P}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:11}{\coqdocvariable{s1}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:12}{\coqdocvariable{s2}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '->' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\rightarrow}}}\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.P:10}{\coqdocvariable{P}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:12}{\coqdocvariable{s2}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:11}{\coqdocvariable{s1}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Proof}. \coqdoctac{unfold} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}}. \coqdoctac{intros} \coqdocvar{X} \coqdocvar{P} \coqdocvar{s1} \coqdocvar{s2} \coqdocvar{H} \coqdocvar{x} \coqdocvar{Px}. \coqdoctac{rewrite} \coqdocvar{H}; \coqdoctac{auto}. \coqdockw{Qed}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Lemma} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.pub equiv trans}{pub\_equiv\_trans}{\coqdoclemma{pub\_equiv\_trans}} : \coqdockw{\ensuremath{\forall}} \{\coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.X:13}{X}{\coqdocbinder{X}}:\coqdockw{Type}\} (\coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.P:14}{P}{\coqdocbinder{P}} : \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub vars}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_vars}}) (\coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:15}{s1}{\coqdocbinder{s1}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:16}{s2}{\coqdocbinder{s2}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s3:17}{s3}{\coqdocbinder{s3}} : \coqref{SECF.Maps.total map}{\coqdocdefinition{total\_map}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.X:13}{\coqdocvariable{X}}),\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.P:14}{\coqdocvariable{P}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:15}{\coqdocvariable{s1}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:16}{\coqdocvariable{s2}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '->' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\rightarrow}}}\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.P:14}{\coqdocvariable{P}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:16}{\coqdocvariable{s2}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s3:17}{\coqdocvariable{s3}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '->' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\rightarrow}}}\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.P:14}{\coqdocvariable{P}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:15}{\coqdocvariable{s1}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s3:17}{\coqdocvariable{s3}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Proof}. \coqdoctac{unfold} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}}. \coqdoctac{intros} \coqdocvar{X} \coqdocvar{P} \coqdocvar{s1} \coqdocvar{s2} \coqdocvar{s3} \coqdocvar{H12} \coqdocvar{H23} \coqdocvar{x} \coqdocvar{Px}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{3.50em}
\coqdoctac{rewrite} \coqdocvar{H12}; \coqdoctac{try} \coqdoctac{rewrite} \coqdocvar{H23}; \coqdoctac{auto}. \coqdockw{Qed}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
Program \coqdocvar{c} is \textit{noninterferent} if whenever it has two terminating
    runs from two publicly equivalent initial states \coqref{SECF.Noninterference.s1}{\coqdocdefinition{s1}} and \coqref{SECF.Noninterference.s2}{\coqdocdefinition{s2}},
    the obtained final states \coqref{SECF.Noninterference.s1'}{\coqdocdefinition{s1'}} and \coqdocvar{s2'} are also publicly equivalent. 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Definition} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.noninterferent}{noninterferent}{\coqdocdefinition{noninterferent}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.P:18}{P}{\coqdocbinder{P}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.c:19}{c}{\coqdocbinder{c}} := \coqdockw{\ensuremath{\forall}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:20}{s1}{\coqdocbinder{s1}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:21}{s2}{\coqdocbinder{s2}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s1':22}{s1'}{\coqdocbinder{s1'}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s2':23}{s2'}{\coqdocbinder{s2'}},\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.P:18}{\coqdocvariable{P}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:20}{\coqdocvariable{s1}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:21}{\coqdocvariable{s2}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '->' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\rightarrow}}}\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:20}{\coqdocvariable{s1}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x}{\coqdocnotation{=[}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.c:19}{\coqdocvariable{c}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x}{\coqdocnotation{]=>}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s1':22}{\coqdocvariable{s1'}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '->' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\rightarrow}}}\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:21}{\coqdocvariable{s2}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x}{\coqdocnotation{=[}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.c:19}{\coqdocvariable{c}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x}{\coqdocnotation{]=>}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s2':23}{\coqdocvariable{s2'}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '->' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\rightarrow}}}\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.P:18}{\coqdocvariable{P}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s1':22}{\coqdocvariable{s1'}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s2':23}{\coqdocvariable{s2'}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
Intuitively, \coqdocvar{c} is noninterferent when the value of the public
    variables in the final state can only depend on the value of
    public variables in the initial state, and do not depend on the
    initial value of secret variables.


    In particular, changing the value of the secret variables in the
    initial state (as allowed by \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.pub equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}} \coqdocvar{P} \coqref{SECF.Noninterference.s1}{\coqdocdefinition{s1}} \coqref{SECF.Noninterference.s2}{\coqdocdefinition{s2}}), should lead to
    no change in the final value of the public variables (as required
    by \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.pub equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}} \coqdocvar{P} \coqref{SECF.Noninterference.s1'}{\coqdocdefinition{s1'}} \coqdocvar{s2'}). 

 For instance, consider the following command
    (taken from \coqdoclibrary{Noninterference}): 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Definition} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.secure com}{secure\_com}{\coqdocdefinition{secure\_com}} : \coqref{SECF.Imp.com}{\coqdocinductive{com}} := \coqref{SECF.Imp.::com scope:'<x7B' x 'x7D>'}{\coqdocnotation{<\{}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:x ':=' x}{\coqdocnotation{:=}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}}\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com::x '+' x}{\coqdocnotation{+}}1\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:x ';' x}{\coqdocnotation{;}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.Y}{\coqdocdefinition{Y}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:x ':=' x}{\coqdocnotation{:=}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}}\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com::x '+' x}{\coqdocnotation{+}}\coqref{SECF.Imp.Y}{\coqdocdefinition{Y}}\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com::x '*' x}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\times}}}2 \coqref{SECF.Imp.::com scope:'<x7B' x 'x7D>'}{\coqdocnotation{\}>}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
If we assume that variable \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}} is public and variable \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.Y}{\coqdocdefinition{Y}} is
    secret, we can state noninterference for \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.secure com}{\coqdocdefinition{secure\_com}} as follows: 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Definition} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.xpub}{xpub}{\coqdocdefinition{xpub}} : \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub vars}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_vars}} := (\coqref{SECF.Imp.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}} \coqref{SECF.Maps.:::x '!->' x ';' x}{\coqdocnotation{!->}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.public}{\coqdocdefinition{public}}\coqref{SECF.Maps.:::x '!->' x ';' x}{\coqdocnotation{;}} \coqref{SECF.Maps.:::'  ' '!->' x}{\coqdocnotation{\_\_}} \coqref{SECF.Maps.:::'  ' '!->' x}{\coqdocnotation{!->}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.secret}{\coqdocdefinition{secret}}).\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Definition} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.noninterferent secure com}{noninterferent\_secure\_com}{\coqdocdefinition{noninterferent\_secure\_com}} :=\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.noninterferent}{\coqdocdefinition{noninterferent}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.xpub}{\coqdocdefinition{xpub}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.secure com}{\coqdocdefinition{secure\_com}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
We have already proved that \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.secure com}{\coqdocdefinition{secure\_com}} is indeed noninterferent
    both directly using the semantics (in \coqdoclibrary{Noninterference}).
    This proof was manual though, while in this chapter we will show
    how this proof can be done more syntactically and automatically
    using several \textit{information-flow-control} (IFC) type systems that
    enforce noninterference for all well-typed programs
    \coqdocvar{Sabelfeld} \coqexternalref{and}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocinductive{and}} \coqdocvar{Myers} 2003 (in Bib.v). 

 Not all programs are noninterferent though. For instance, a
    program that reads the contents of a secret variable and uses that
    to change the value of a public variable is unlikely to be
    noninterferent. We call this an \textit{explicit flow} and all our type
    systems will prevent \textit{all} explicit flows.


    Here is a program that has an explicit flow, which in this case
    breaks noninterference (as we proved in \coqdoclibrary{Noninterference}): 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Definition} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.insecure com1}{insecure\_com1}{\coqdocdefinition{insecure\_com1}} : \coqref{SECF.Imp.com}{\coqdocinductive{com}} :=\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.Imp.::com scope:'<x7B' x 'x7D>'}{\coqdocnotation{<\{}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:x ':=' x}{\coqdocnotation{:=}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.Y}{\coqdocdefinition{Y}}\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com::x '+' x}{\coqdocnotation{+}}1\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:x ';' x}{\coqdocnotation{;}} \coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{2.50em}
\coqref{SECF.Imp.Y}{\coqdocdefinition{Y}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:x ':=' x}{\coqdocnotation{:=}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}}\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com::x '+' x}{\coqdocnotation{+}}\coqref{SECF.Imp.Y}{\coqdocdefinition{Y}}\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com::x '*' x}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\times}}}2 \coqref{SECF.Imp.::com scope:'<x7B' x 'x7D>'}{\coqdocnotation{\}>}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Definition} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.interferent insecure com1}{interferent\_insecure\_com1}{\coqdocdefinition{interferent\_insecure\_com1}} :=\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqexternalref{::type scope:'x7E' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\lnot}}}\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.noninterferent}{\coqdocdefinition{noninterferent}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.xpub}{\coqdocdefinition{xpub}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.insecure com1}{\coqdocdefinition{insecure\_com1}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
Not all explicit flows break noninterference though. For instance,
    the following variant of \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.insecure com1}{\coqdocdefinition{insecure\_com1}} is noninterferent even if
    it has an explicit flow. The reason for this is that the variable
    \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}} is overwritten with public information in a subsequent assignment. 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Definition} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.secure com1'}{secure\_com1'}{\coqdocdefinition{secure\_com1'}} : \coqref{SECF.Imp.com}{\coqdocinductive{com}} :=\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.Imp.::com scope:'<x7B' x 'x7D>'}{\coqdocnotation{<\{}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:x ':=' x}{\coqdocnotation{:=}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.Y}{\coqdocdefinition{Y}}\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com::x '+' x}{\coqdocnotation{+}}1\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:x ';' x}{\coqdocnotation{;}} \coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{2.50em}
\coqref{SECF.Imp.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:x ':=' x}{\coqdocnotation{:=}} 42\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:x ';' x}{\coqdocnotation{;}} \coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{2.50em}
\coqref{SECF.Imp.Y}{\coqdocdefinition{Y}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:x ':=' x}{\coqdocnotation{:=}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}}\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com::x '+' x}{\coqdocnotation{+}}\coqref{SECF.Imp.Y}{\coqdocdefinition{Y}}\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com::x '*' x}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\times}}}2 \coqref{SECF.Imp.::com scope:'<x7B' x 'x7D>'}{\coqdocnotation{\}>}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
Since our IFC type systems will prevent all explicit flows, they
    will also reject \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.secure com1'}{\coqdocdefinition{secure\_com1'}}, even if it is secure with respect
    to our simple attacker model for noninterference, in which the
    attacker only observes the \textit{final} values of public variables.


    Our type systems will only provide \textit{sound syntactic
    overapproximations} of the semantic noninterference property. 

 Explicit flows are not the only way to leak secrets: one can also
    leak secrets using the control flow of the program, by branching
    on secrets and then assigning to public variables. We call these
    leaks \textit{implicit flows}. 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Definition} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.insecure com2}{insecure\_com2}{\coqdocdefinition{insecure\_com2}} : \coqref{SECF.Imp.com}{\coqdocinductive{com}} :=\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.Imp.::com scope:'<x7B' x 'x7D>'}{\coqdocnotation{<\{}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:'if' x 'then' x 'else' x 'end'}{\coqdocnotation{if}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.Y}{\coqdocdefinition{Y}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:com::x '=' x}{\coqdocnotation{=}} 0\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{2.50em}
\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:'if' x 'then' x 'else' x 'end'}{\coqdocnotation{then}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.Y}{\coqdocdefinition{Y}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:x ':=' x}{\coqdocnotation{:=}} 42\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{2.50em}
\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:'if' x 'then' x 'else' x 'end'}{\coqdocnotation{else}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:x ':=' x}{\coqdocnotation{:=}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}}\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com::x '+' x}{\coqdocnotation{+}}1 \coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{2.50em}
\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:'if' x 'then' x 'else' x 'end'}{\coqdocnotation{end}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.::com scope:'<x7B' x 'x7D>'}{\coqdocnotation{\}>}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
Here the expression \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}}+1 we are assigning to \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}} is public
    information, but we are doing this assignment after we branched on
    a secret condition \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.Y}{\coqdocdefinition{Y}} = 0, so we are indirectly leaking
    information about the value of \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.Y}{\coqdocdefinition{Y}}. In this case we can infer that
    if \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}} gets incremented the value of \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.Y}{\coqdocdefinition{Y}} is not 0. This program
    is insecure (as proved in \coqdoclibrary{Noninterference}), so it will be
    rejected by our type systems, which enforce noninterference by
    also preventing \textit{all} implicit flows. 

 Not all implicit flows break noninterference though. Here is a
    program that is noninterferent, even though it contains both an
    explicit and an implicit flow: 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Definition} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.secure p2}{secure\_p2}{\coqdocdefinition{secure\_p2}} :=\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.Imp.::com scope:'<x7B' x 'x7D>'}{\coqdocnotation{<\{}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:'if' x 'then' x 'else' x 'end'}{\coqdocnotation{if}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.Y}{\coqdocdefinition{Y}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:com::x '=' x}{\coqdocnotation{=}} 0\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{2.50em}
\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:'if' x 'then' x 'else' x 'end'}{\coqdocnotation{then}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:x ':=' x}{\coqdocnotation{:=}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.Y}{\coqdocdefinition{Y}} \coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{2.50em}
\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:'if' x 'then' x 'else' x 'end'}{\coqdocnotation{else}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:x ':=' x}{\coqdocnotation{:=}} 0 \coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{2.50em}
\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:'if' x 'then' x 'else' x 'end'}{\coqdocnotation{end}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.::com scope:'<x7B' x 'x7D>'}{\coqdocnotation{\}>}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
Intuitively, even if this program branches on the secret \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.Y}{\coqdocdefinition{Y}}, it
    always assigns the value 0 to \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}}, so no secret is
    leaked. We can prove this semantically: 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Lemma} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.xpub true}{xpub\_true}{\coqdoclemma{xpub\_true}} : \coqdockw{\ensuremath{\forall}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.x:24}{x}{\coqdocbinder{x}}, \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.xpub}{\coqdocdefinition{xpub}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.x:24}{\coqdocvariable{x}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '=' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{=}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:::'true'}{\coqdocnotation{true}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '->' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\rightarrow}}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.x:24}{\coqdocvariable{x}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '=' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{=}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Proof}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqdoctac{unfold} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.xpub}{\coqdocdefinition{xpub}}. \coqdoctac{intros} \coqdocvar{x} \coqdocvar{Hx}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqdoctac{destruct} (\coqdoclemma{String.eqb\_spec} \coqdocvar{x} \coqref{SECF.Imp.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}}).\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
- \coqdoctac{subst}. \coqdoctac{reflexivity}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
- \coqdoctac{rewrite} \coqref{SECF.Maps.t update neq}{\coqdocaxiom{t\_update\_neq}} \coqdoctac{in} \coqdocvar{Hx}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{2.00em}
+ \coqdoctac{rewrite} \coqref{SECF.Maps.t apply empty}{\coqdocaxiom{t\_apply\_empty}} \coqdoctac{in} \coqdocvar{Hx}. \coqdoctac{discriminate}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{2.00em}
+ \coqdoctac{intro} \coqdocvar{contra}. \coqdoctac{subst}. \coqdocvar{contradiction}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Qed}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Ltac} \coqdocvar{invert} \coqdocvar{H} := \coqdoctac{inversion} \coqdocvar{H}; \coqdoctac{subst}; \coqdoctac{clear} \coqdocvar{H}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Lemma} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.noninterference secure p2}{noninterference\_secure\_p2}{\coqdoclemma{noninterference\_secure\_p2}} :\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.noninterferent}{\coqdocdefinition{noninterferent}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.xpub}{\coqdocdefinition{xpub}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.secure p2}{\coqdocdefinition{secure\_p2}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Proof}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqdoctac{unfold} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.noninterferent}{\coqdocdefinition{noninterferent}}, \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.secure p2}{\coqdocdefinition{secure\_p2}}, \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqdoctac{intros} \coqdocvar{s1} \coqdocvar{s2} \coqdocvar{s1'} \coqdocvar{s2'} \coqdocvar{H} \coqdocvar{H1} \coqdocvar{H2} \coqdocvar{x} \coqdocvar{Hx}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqdoctac{apply} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.xpub true}{\coqdoclemma{xpub\_true}} \coqdoctac{in} \coqdocvar{Hx}. \coqdoctac{subst}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqdocvar{invert} \coqdocvar{H1}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
- \coqdocvar{invert} \coqdocvar{H2}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{2.00em}
+ \coqdocvar{invert} \coqdocvar{H8}. \coqdocvar{invert} \coqdocvar{H9}. \coqdoctac{simpl}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{3.00em}
\coqdoctac{repeat} \coqdoctac{rewrite} \coqref{SECF.Maps.t update eq}{\coqdocaxiom{t\_update\_eq}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{3.00em}
\coqdocvar{invert} \coqdocvar{H7}. \coqdocvar{invert} \coqdocvar{H6}.\coqdoceol

\coqdoctac{repeat} \coqdoctac{rewrite} \coqdoclemma{Nat.eqb\_eq} \coqdoctac{in} *. \coqdoctac{rewrite} \coqdocvar{H1}, \coqdocvar{H2}. \coqdoctac{auto}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{2.00em}
+ \coqdocvar{invert} \coqdocvar{H8}. \coqdocvar{invert} \coqdocvar{H9}. \coqdoctac{simpl}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{3.00em}
\coqdoctac{repeat} \coqdoctac{rewrite} \coqref{SECF.Maps.t update eq}{\coqdocaxiom{t\_update\_eq}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{3.00em}
\coqdocvar{invert} \coqdocvar{H7}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{3.00em}
\coqdoctac{rewrite} \coqdoclemma{Nat.eqb\_eq} \coqdoctac{in} \coqdocvar{H1}. \coqdoctac{auto}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
- \coqdocvar{invert} \coqdocvar{H2}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{2.00em}
+ \coqdocvar{invert} \coqdocvar{H8}. \coqdocvar{invert} \coqdocvar{H9}. \coqdoctac{simpl}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{3.00em}
\coqdoctac{repeat} \coqdoctac{rewrite} \coqref{SECF.Maps.t update eq}{\coqdocaxiom{t\_update\_eq}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{3.00em}
\coqdocvar{invert} \coqdocvar{H6}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{3.00em}
\coqdoctac{rewrite} \coqdoclemma{Nat.eqb\_eq} \coqdoctac{in} \coqdocvar{H1}. \coqdoctac{auto}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{2.00em}
+ \coqdocvar{invert} \coqdocvar{H8}. \coqdocvar{invert} \coqdocvar{H9}. \coqdoctac{simpl}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{3.00em}
\coqdoctac{repeat} \coqdoctac{rewrite} \coqref{SECF.Maps.t update eq}{\coqdocaxiom{t\_update\_eq}}. \coqdoctac{auto}.\coqdoceol

\coqdockw{Qed}.\coqdoceol

Still, our type systems will reject programs containing
    any explicit or implicit flows, this one included. C'est la vie! 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
\section{Type system for noninterference of expressions}



 We will build a type system that prevents all explicit and
    implicit flows.


    But first, let's start with something simpler, a type system for
    arithmetic expressions: our typing judgement \coqdocvar{P} \ensuremath{\vdash}\coqdocvar{a}- \coqdocvar{a} \symbol{92}\coqdoctac{in} \coqdocvar{l}
    specifies the label \coqdocvar{l} of an arithmetic expression \coqdocvar{a} in terms
    of the labels of the variables read it reads.


    In particular, \coqdocvar{P} \ensuremath{\vdash}\coqdocvar{a}- \coqdocvar{a} \symbol{92}\coqdoctac{in} \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.public}{\coqdocdefinition{public}} says that expression \coqdocvar{a}
    only reads public variables, so it computes a public value.
    \coqdocvar{P} \ensuremath{\vdash}\coqdocvar{a}- \coqdocvar{a} \symbol{92}\coqdoctac{in} \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.secret}{\coqdocdefinition{secret}} says that expression \coqdocvar{a} reads some secret
    variable, so it computes a value that may depend on secrets. 

 Here are some examples:

\begin{itemize}
\item  For a variable \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}} we just look up its label in P, so
      \coqdocvar{P} \ensuremath{\vdash}\coqdocvar{a}- \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}} \symbol{92}\coqdoctac{in} (\coqdocvar{P} \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}}).

\item  For a constant \coqdocvar{n} the label is \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.public}{\coqdocdefinition{public}}, so
      \coqdocvar{P} \ensuremath{\vdash}\coqdocvar{a} \coqdocvar{n} \symbol{92}\coqdoctac{in} \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.public}{\coqdocdefinition{public}}.

\item  Given variable \coqdocvar{X1} with label \coqdocvar{l1} and variable \coqdocvar{X2} with
      label \coqdocvar{l2}, what should be the label of \coqdocvar{X1} + \coqdocvar{X2} though? 

\end{itemize}
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
\subsection{Combining labels}



 We need a way to combine the labels of two sub-expressions, which
    we call the \textit{join} (or least upper bound) of the two labels: 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Definition} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.join}{join}{\coqdocdefinition{join}} (\coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.l1:25}{l1}{\coqdocbinder{l1}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.l2:26}{l2}{\coqdocbinder{l2}} : \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.label}{\coqdocdefinition{label}}) : \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.label}{\coqdocdefinition{label}} := \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.l1:25}{\coqdocvariable{l1}} \coqexternalref{::bool scope:x 'x26x26' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Datatypes}{\coqdocnotation{\&\&}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.l2:26}{\coqdocvariable{l2}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
Intuitively, if we add up two expressions \coqdocvar{e1} labeled \coqdocvar{l1} and
    \coqdocvar{e2} labeled \coqdocvar{l2}, the result of the addition will be labeled
    \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.join}{\coqdocdefinition{join}} \coqdocvar{l1} \coqdocvar{l2}, which is public iff \coqdocvar{l1} is public \textit{and} \coqdocvar{l2} is public. 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Lemma} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.join commutative}{join\_commutative}{\coqdoclemma{join\_commutative}} : \coqdockw{\ensuremath{\forall}} \{\coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.l1:27}{l1}{\coqdocbinder{l1}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.l2:28}{l2}{\coqdocbinder{l2}}\},\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.join}{\coqdocdefinition{join}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.l1:27}{\coqdocvariable{l1}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.l2:28}{\coqdocvariable{l2}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '=' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{=}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.join}{\coqdocdefinition{join}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.l2:28}{\coqdocvariable{l2}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.l1:27}{\coqdocvariable{l1}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Proof}. \coqdoctac{intros} \coqdocvar{l1} \coqdocvar{l2}. \coqdoctac{destruct} \coqdocvar{l1}; \coqdoctac{destruct} \coqdocvar{l2}; \coqdoctac{reflexivity}. \coqdockw{Qed}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Lemma} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.join public}{join\_public}{\coqdoclemma{join\_public}} : \coqdockw{\ensuremath{\forall}} \{\coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.l1:29}{l1}{\coqdocbinder{l1}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.l2:30}{l2}{\coqdocbinder{l2}}\},\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.join}{\coqdocdefinition{join}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.l1:29}{\coqdocvariable{l1}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.l2:30}{\coqdocvariable{l2}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '=' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{=}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.public}{\coqdocdefinition{public}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '->' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\rightarrow}}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.l1:29}{\coqdocvariable{l1}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '=' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{=}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.public}{\coqdocdefinition{public}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '/x5C' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\land}}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.l2:30}{\coqdocvariable{l2}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '=' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{=}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.public}{\coqdocdefinition{public}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Proof}. \coqdoctac{apply} \coqexternalref{andb prop}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Datatypes}{\coqdoclemma{andb\_prop}}. \coqdockw{Qed}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Lemma} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.join public l}{join\_public\_l}{\coqdoclemma{join\_public\_l}} : \coqdockw{\ensuremath{\forall}} \{\coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.l:31}{l}{\coqdocbinder{l}}\},\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.join}{\coqdocdefinition{join}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.public}{\coqdocdefinition{public}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.l:31}{\coqdocvariable{l}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '=' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{=}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.l:31}{\coqdocvariable{l}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Proof}. \coqdoctac{reflexivity}. \coqdockw{Qed}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Lemma} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.join public r}{join\_public\_r}{\coqdoclemma{join\_public\_r}} : \coqdockw{\ensuremath{\forall}} \{\coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.l:32}{l}{\coqdocbinder{l}}\},\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.join}{\coqdocdefinition{join}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.l:32}{\coqdocvariable{l}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.public}{\coqdocdefinition{public}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '=' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{=}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.l:32}{\coqdocvariable{l}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Proof}. \coqdoctac{intros} \coqdocvar{l}. \coqdoctac{rewrite} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.join commutative}{\coqdoclemma{join\_commutative}}. \coqdoctac{reflexivity}. \coqdockw{Qed}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Lemma} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.join secret l}{join\_secret\_l}{\coqdoclemma{join\_secret\_l}} : \coqdockw{\ensuremath{\forall}} \{\coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.l:33}{l}{\coqdocbinder{l}}\},\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.join}{\coqdocdefinition{join}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.secret}{\coqdocdefinition{secret}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.l:33}{\coqdocvariable{l}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '=' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{=}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.secret}{\coqdocdefinition{secret}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocnoindent

We now define a set of rules for the IFC typing relation for
    arithmetic expressions \coqdocvar{P} \ensuremath{\vdash}\coqdocvar{a}- \coqdocvar{a} \symbol{92}\coqdoctac{in} \coqdocvar{l}, which we read as follows:
    ``given the public variables \coqdocvar{P} expression \coqdocvar{a} has label \coqdocvar{l}:'' 

\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}                  (T\_Num)
                          P |-a- n \symbol{92}in public
\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}                    (T\_Id)
                           P |-a- X \symbol{92}in P X


                  P |-a- a1 \symbol{92}in l1    P |-a- a2 \symbol{92}in l2
\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}        (T\_Plus)
                      P |-a- a1+a2 \symbol{92}in join l1 l2


                  P |-a- a1 \symbol{92}in l1    P |-a- a2 \symbol{92}in l2
\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}       (T\_Minus)
                      P |-a- a1-a2 \symbol{92}in join l1 l2


                  P |-a- a1 \symbol{92}in l1    P |-a- a2 \symbol{92}in l2
\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}        (T\_Mult)
                      P |-a- a1*a2 \symbol{92}in join l1 l2

\subsection{Computing labels of arithmetic expressions}



 Beyond \textit{specifying} when an expression has a certain label as an
    inductive relation, we can also easily \textit{compute} the label of an
    expression:

\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqdockw{match} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.a:53}{\coqdocvariable{a}} \coqdockw{with}\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}

\coqdocindent{1.00em}

\coqdocindent{1.00em}

\coqdocindent{1.00em}

\section{Restrictive type system prohibiting branching on secrets}



 For commands, we start with a simple type system that doesn't
    allow any branching on secrets, which is so strong that on its own
    prevents all implicit flows. 

 For preventing explicit flows when typing assignments, we need to
    define when it is okay for information to flow from an expression
    with label \coqdocvar{l1} to a variable with label \coqdocvar{l1}. 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Definition} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.can flow}{can\_flow}{\coqdocdefinition{can\_flow}} (\coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.l1:103}{l1}{\coqdocbinder{l1}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.l2:104}{l2}{\coqdocbinder{l2}} : \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.label}{\coqdocdefinition{label}}) : \coqexternalref{bool}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Datatypes}{\coqdocinductive{bool}} := \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.l1:103}{\coqdocvariable{l1}} \coqexternalref{::bool scope:x '||' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Datatypes}{\coqdocnotation{||}} \coqexternalref{negb}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Datatypes}{\coqdocdefinition{negb}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.l2:104}{\coqdocvariable{l2}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
One way to read this is as boolean implication from \coqdocvar{l2} to \coqdocvar{l1},
    so \coqdocvar{l1} can flow to \coqdocvar{l2} iff \coqdocvar{l2} is public implies that \coqdocvar{l1} is
    public as well. In particular, this disallows that the value of
    secret expressions be assigned to public variables: 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Lemma} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.cannot flow secret public}{cannot\_flow\_secret\_public}{\coqdoclemma{cannot\_flow\_secret\_public}} : \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.can flow}{\coqdocdefinition{can\_flow}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.secret}{\coqdocdefinition{secret}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.public}{\coqdocdefinition{public}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '=' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{=}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:::'false'}{\coqdocnotation{false}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Proof}. \coqdoctac{reflexivity}. \coqdockw{Qed}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
This allows public information to flow everywhere, and secret
    information to flow to secret variables:

For commands we use the previous relations to define a
    \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.cf well typed}{\coqdocinductive{cf\_well\_typed}} relation inductively using the following rules: 

\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}                    (CFWT\_Skip)
                            P |-cf- skip


             P |-a- a \symbol{92}in l    can\_flow l (P X) = true
\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}      (CFWT\_Asgn)
                           P |-cf- X := a


                      P |-cf- c1    P |-cf- c2
\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}               (CFWT\_Seq)
                            P |-cf- c1;c2


           P |-b- b \symbol{92}in public    P |-cf- c1    P |-cf- c2
\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}    (CFWT\_If)
                      P |-cf- if b then c1 else c2


                  P |-b- b \symbol{92}in public    P |-cf- c
\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}         (CFWT\_While)
                    P |-cf- while b then c end


 Intuitively, explicit flows are prevented by the \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.can flow}{\coqdocdefinition{can\_flow}}
    requirement in the assignment rule and implicit flows are
    prevented by the requirement that the boolean condition of \coqdockw{if}
    and \coqdocvar{while} has to be a public expression.

It is worth noting that, while our type-checker is sound and
    complete wrt the \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.cf well typed}{\coqdocinductive{cf\_well\_typed}} relation, this relation is only a
    sound overapproximation of noninterference (proved below), but not
    complete. So the type-checker is also not complete wrt
    noninterference, but is still provides an efficient way of proving
    it. For a start, let's use the type-checker to prove or disprove the
    \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.cf well typed}{\coqdocinductive{cf\_well\_typed}} relation for concrete programs by computation: 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}

\subsection{Noninterference enforced by \texorpdfstring{\protect\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.cf well typed}{\coqdocinductive{cf\_well\_typed}}}{cf\_well\_typed}}

\coqdoctac{rewrite} \coqdocvar{H} \coqdoctac{in} \coqdocvar{H4}. \coqdoctac{discriminate} \coqdocvar{H4}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
- \coqdoctac{eapply} \coqdocvar{IHHeval1\_2}; \coqdoctac{try} \coqdocvar{eassumption}. \coqdoctac{eapply} \coqdocvar{IHHeval1\_1}; \coqdocvar{eassumption}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Qed}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
Remember the definition of \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.OUTPUT.noninterferent}{\coqdocdefinition{noninterferent}} is as follows:


forall s1 s2 s1' s2',
  pub\_equiv P s1 s2 ->
  s1 = \coqdocvar{c} => s1' ->
  s2 = \coqdocvar{c} => s2' ->
  pub\_equiv P s1' s2'.


   The main intuition is that the two executions will proceed ``in
   lockstep'', because all the branch conditions are enforced to be
   public, so they will execute to the same Boolean in both executions. 

 The proof is by induction on \coqref{SECF.Noninterference.s1}{\coqdocdefinition{s1}} =[ \coqdocvar{c} ]=> \coqref{SECF.Noninterference.s1'}{\coqdocdefinition{s1'}} and inversion
    on \coqref{SECF.Noninterference.s2}{\coqdocdefinition{s2}} =[ \coqdocvar{c} ]=> \coqdocvar{s2'} and \coqdocvar{P} \ensuremath{\vdash}\coqdocvar{cf}- \coqdocvar{c}. Here is a sketch of the two
    most interesting cases:



\begin{itemize}
\item  In the conditional case we have that \coqdocvar{c} is \coqdockw{if} \coqdocvar{b} \coqdockw{then} \coqdocvar{c1} \coqdockw{else} \coqdocvar{c2},
      \coqdocvar{P} \ensuremath{\vdash}\coqdocvar{cf}- \coqdocvar{c1}, \coqdocvar{P} \ensuremath{\vdash}\coqdocvar{cf}- \coqdocvar{c2}, and \coqdocvar{P} \ensuremath{\vdash}\coqdocvar{b}- \coqdocvar{b} \symbol{92}\coqdoctac{in} \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.public}{\coqdocdefinition{public}}. Given this
      last fact we can apply noninterference of boolean expressions to
      show that \coqref{SECF.Imp.beval}{\coqdocdefinition{beval}} \coqdocvar{st1} \coqdocvar{b} = \coqref{SECF.Imp.beval}{\coqdocdefinition{beval}} \coqdocvar{st2} \coqdocvar{b}. If they are both \coqexternalref{true}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Datatypes}{\coqdocconstructor{true}},
      we use the induction hypothesis for \coqdocvar{c1}, and if they are both
      false we use the induction hypothesis for \coqdocvar{c2} to conclude.



\item  In the assignment case we have that \coqdocvar{c} is \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}} := \coqdocvar{a},
      \coqdocvar{P} \ensuremath{\vdash}\coqdocvar{a}- \coqdocvar{a} \symbol{92}\coqdoctac{in} \coqdocvar{l}, and \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.can flow}{\coqdocdefinition{can\_flow}} \coqdocvar{l} (\coqdocvar{P} \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}}) = \coqexternalref{true}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Datatypes}{\coqdocconstructor{true}}, which expands out
      to \coqdocvar{l} == \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.public}{\coqdocdefinition{public}} \ensuremath{\lor} \coqdocvar{P} \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}} == \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.secret}{\coqdocdefinition{secret}}.


      If \coqdocvar{l} == \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.public}{\coqdocdefinition{public}} then by noninterference of arithmetic
      expressions then \coqref{SECF.Imp.aeval}{\coqdocdefinition{aeval}} \coqdocvar{st1} \coqdocvar{a} = \coqref{SECF.Imp.aeval}{\coqdocdefinition{aeval}} \coqref{SECF.Noninterference.s2}{\coqdocdefinition{s2}} \coqdocvar{a}, so we are
      assigning the same value to X, which leads to public equivalent
      final states (since the initial states were public equivalent).


      If \coqdocvar{P} \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}} == \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.secret}{\coqdocdefinition{secret}} then the value of \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.X}{\coqdocdefinition{X}} doesn't matter
      for determining whether the final states are \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.pub equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}}. 

\end{itemize}
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
\subsection{\texorpdfstring{\protect\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.cf well typed}{\coqdocinductive{cf\_well\_typed}}}{cf\_well\_typed} too strong for noninterference}



 While we have just proved that \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.cf well typed}{\coqdocinductive{cf\_well\_typed}} implies
    noninterference, this type system is too restrictive for enforcing just
    noninterference. For instance, the following program is rejected
    by the type system just because it branches on a secret: 

\paragraph{Exercise: 1 star, standard (not\_cf\_wt\_noninterferent\_com)}



 Use the type-checker to prove that the following program is
    not \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.cf well typed}{\coqdocinductive{cf\_well\_typed}} (Hint: This can be proved very easily, if
    stuck see examples above): 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Example} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.not cf wt noninterferent com}{not\_cf\_wt\_noninterferent\_com}{\coqdocdefinition{not\_cf\_wt\_noninterferent\_com}} :\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqexternalref{::type scope:'x7E' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\lnot}}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.xpub}{\coqdocdefinition{xpub}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x '|-cf-' x}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\vdash}}}\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x '|-cf-' x}{\coqdocnotation{cf}}\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x '|-cf-' x}{\coqdocnotation{-}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.::com scope:'<x7B' x 'x7D>'}{\coqdocnotation{<\{}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:'if' x 'then' x 'else' x 'end'}{\coqdocnotation{if}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.Y}{\coqdocdefinition{Y}}\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com::x '=' x}{\coqdocnotation{=}}0 \coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{8.50em}
\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:'if' x 'then' x 'else' x 'end'}{\coqdocnotation{then}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.Z}{\coqdocdefinition{Z}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:x ':=' x}{\coqdocnotation{:=}} 0\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{8.50em}
\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:'if' x 'then' x 'else' x 'end'}{\coqdocnotation{else}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:'skip'}{\coqdocnotation{skip}}\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{8.50em}
\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:'if' x 'then' x 'else' x 'end'}{\coqdocnotation{end}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.::com scope:'<x7B' x 'x7D>'}{\coqdocnotation{\}>}}.\coqdoceol

 \coqdocvar{Admitted}.\coqdoceol

\ensuremath{\Box} 

 Yet this program contains no explicit flows and no implicit flows
    (since the assigned variable \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.Z}{\coqdocdefinition{Z}} is secret), so it is intuitively
    noninterferent, and with a bit more work we can prove this formally:

We will later show that \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.cf well typed}{\coqdocinductive{cf\_well\_typed}} enforces not just
    noninterference, but also a security notion called Control Flow
    security, which prevents some side-channel attacks and which also
    serves as the base for cryptographic constant-time. 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
\section{IFC type system allowing branching on secrets}



 Let's now investigate a more permissive type system for
    noninterference in which we do allow branching on secrets
    \coqdocvar{Volpano} \coqdocvar{et} \coqdocvar{al} 1996 (in Bib.v).


    Now to prevent implicit flows we need to track whether we have
    branched on secrets. We do this with a \textit{program counter} (\coqdocvar{pc})
    label, which records the labels of the branches we have taken at
    the current point in the execution (joined together). 

\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}                      (NIWT\_Skip)
                      P ;; pc |-ni- skip


       P |-a- a \symbol{92}in l   can\_flow (join pc l) (P X) = true
\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}   (NIWT\_Asgn)
                     P ;; pc |-ni- X := a


                P ;; pc |-ni- c1    P ;; pc |-ni- c2
\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}             (NIWT\_Seq)
                      P ;; pc |-ni- c1;c2


           P |-b- b \symbol{92}in l    P ;; join pc l |-ni- c1
                             P ;; join pc l |-ni- c2
\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}            (NIWT\_If)
                P ;; pc |-ni- if b then c1 else c2


              P |-b- b \symbol{92}in l    P ;; join pc l |-ni- c
\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}       (NIWT\_While)
                P ;; pc |-ni- while b then c end

\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent

We now allow branching on arbitrary boolean expressions in \coqdockw{if}
    and \coqdocvar{while}, but join the label of the branch expression to the
    \coqdocvar{pc}. Then in the assignment rule we require that also the \coqdocvar{pc}
    label flows to the label of the assigned variable, in order to
    still prevent implicit flows.

\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}

With this more permissive type system we can accept more
    noninterferent programs that were rejected by \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.cf well typed}{\coqdocinductive{cf\_well\_typed}}. 
\begin{coqdoccode}

\coqdocnoindent

\coqdocindent{1.00em}

\subsection{Dealing with unsynchronized executions running different code}



 The \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.OUTPUT.different code}{\coqdoclemma{different\_code}} corollary below is crucial for proving that
    the type system above still enforces noninterference even if it
    allows branching on secrets, and its proof follows easily from the
    following basic lemma:

\begin{coqdoccode}

\coqdocindent{1.00em}

The noninterference proof is still relatively simple, since the
    cases in which we take different branches based on secret
    information are all handled by the \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.OUTPUT.different code}{\coqdoclemma{different\_code}} lemma.


    Another key ingredient for having a simple noninterference proof
    is working with a big-step semantics for Imp. 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
\section{Type system for termination-sensitive noninterference}



 The noninterference notion we used above was ``termination
    insensitive''. If we prevent loop conditions depending on secrets
    we can actually enforce termination-sensitive noninterference
    (TSNI), which we defined in \coqdoclibrary{Noninterference} as follows:

\coqdocnoindent

\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqdockw{\ensuremath{\forall}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:198}{s1}{\coqdocbinder{s1}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:199}{s2}{\coqdocbinder{s2}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s1':200}{s1'}{\coqdocbinder{s1'}},\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:198}{\coqdocvariable{s1}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x}{\coqdocnotation{=[}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.c:197}{\coqdocvariable{c}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x}{\coqdocnotation{]=>}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s1':200}{\coqdocvariable{s1'}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '->' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\rightarrow}}}\coqdoceol

\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqexternalref{::type scope:x '->' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{(}}\coqexternalref{::type scope:'exists' x '..' x ',' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\exists}}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s2':201}{s2'}{\coqdocbinder{s2'}}\coqexternalref{::type scope:'exists' x '..' x ',' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{,}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:199}{\coqdocvariable{s2}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x}{\coqdocnotation{=[}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.c:197}{\coqdocvariable{c}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x}{\coqdocnotation{]=>}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s2':201}{\coqdocvariable{s2'}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '/x5C' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\land}}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.P:196}{\coqdocvariable{P}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s1':200}{\coqdocvariable{s1'}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s2':201}{\coqdocvariable{s2'}}\coqexternalref{::type scope:x '->' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{)}}.\coqdoceol

We could prove that \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.cf well typed}{\coqdocinductive{cf\_well\_typed}} enforces TSNI, but that typing
    relation is too restrictive, since for TSNI we can allow
    if-then-else conditions to depend on secrets. So we define another
    type system that only prevents \textit{loop} conditions from depending on
    secrets \coqdocvar{Volpano} \coqexternalref{and}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocinductive{and}} \coqdocvar{Smith} 1997 (in Bib.v). 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
\subsection{We just need to update the while rule of \texorpdfstring{\protect\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.ni well typed}{\coqdocinductive{ni\_well\_typed}}}{ni\_well\_typed}:}



 Old rule for noninterference:


              P |-b- b \symbol{92}in l    P ;; join pc l |-ni- c
\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}       (NIWT\_While)
                P ;; pc |-ni- while b then c end


    New rule for termination-sensitive noninterference:


          P |-b- b \symbol{92}in public    P ;; public |-ts- c
\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}       (TSWT\_While)
             P ;; public |-ts- while b then c end


   Beyond requiring the label of \coqdocvar{b} to be \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.public}{\coqdocdefinition{public}}, this rule also
   requires that once one branches on secrets with if-then-else
   (i.e. pc=secret) no while loops are allowed.

\subsection{TSNI Type-Checker}



 In the following exercises you will write a type-checker for the TSNI type
    system above and prove your type-checker sound and complete.

 With this termination-sensitive type-checker, we reject programs
    where the termination behavior itself leaks secret information.
    The following example shows a command that either runs forever or
    terminates depending on the value of a secret variable (Y). 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Definition} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.termination leak}{termination\_leak}{\coqdocdefinition{termination\_leak}} : \coqref{SECF.Imp.com}{\coqdocinductive{com}} :=\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{2.00em}
\coqref{SECF.Imp.::com scope:'<x7B' x 'x7D>'}{\coqdocnotation{<\{}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.:com:com scope:'if' x 'then' x 'else' x 'end'}{\coqdocnotation{if}} \coqref{SECF.Imp.Y}{\coqdocdefinition{Y}}\coqref{SECF.Imp.:com::x '=' x}{\coqdocnotation{=}}0                        \coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{3.50em}

Our previous termination-insensitive type system accepts this program: 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Example} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.ni termination leak}{ni\_termination\_leak}{\coqdocdefinition{ni\_termination\_leak}} :\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.xpub}{\coqdocdefinition{xpub}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x ';;' x '|-ni-' x}{\coqdocnotation{;;}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.public}{\coqdocdefinition{public}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x ';;' x '|-ni-' x}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\vdash}}}\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x ';;' x '|-ni-' x}{\coqdocnotation{ni}}\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x ';;' x '|-ni-' x}{\coqdocnotation{-}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.termination leak}{\coqdocdefinition{termination\_leak}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Proof}. \coqdoctac{apply} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.ni typechecker sound}{\coqdoclemma{ni\_typechecker\_sound}}. \coqdoctac{reflexivity}. \coqdockw{Qed}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
But our new termination-sensitive type system rejects it,
    and you can use your new type-checker to prove it: 

\paragraph{Exercise: 1 star, standard (not\_ts\_non\_termination\_com)}


\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocnoindent

\coqdocindent{1.00em}

\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Proof}.\coqdoceol
 \coqdocvar{Admitted}.\coqdoceol
\end{coqdoccode}
\ensuremath{\Box} 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
\subsection{We prove that \texorpdfstring{\protect\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.ts well typed}{\coqdocinductive{ts\_well\_typed}}}{ts\_well\_typed} enforces TSNI.}



 For this we show that \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.ts well typed}{\coqdocinductive{ts\_well\_typed}} implies \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.ni well typed}{\coqdocinductive{ni\_well\_typed}}, so
    by our previous theorem also (termination-insensitive) \coqdocvar{noninterference}.


    Then we show that \coqdocvar{P};; \coqref{SECF.SpecCT.secret}{\coqdocdefinition{secret}} \ensuremath{\vdash}\coqdocvar{ts}- \coqdocvar{c} implies termination.


    We use this to show that \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.ts well typed}{\coqdocinductive{ts\_well\_typed}} implies equitermination, which
    together with noninterference implies termination-sensitive noninterference.

\section{Control Flow security}



 Especially for cryptographic code one is also worried about
    side-channel attacks, in which secrets are for instance leaked via
    the execution time of the program. For instance, most processors
    have instruction caches, which make executing cached instructions
    faster than non-cached ones.


    To prevent such attacks, cryptographic code is normally written
    without branching on any secrets. To formalize this we introduce a
    security notion called \textit{Control Flow (CF) security}
    (sometimes called PC security \coqdocvar{Molnar} \coqdocvar{et} \coqdocvar{al} 2005 (in Bib.v)), which
    considers the program's branching visible to the attacker. More
    precisely, we instrument the operational semantics of \coqdoclibrary{Imp} to
    also record the control-flow decisions of the program. 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Definition} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.branches}{branches}{\coqdocdefinition{branches}} := \coqexternalref{list}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Datatypes}{\coqdocinductive{list}} \coqexternalref{bool}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Datatypes}{\coqdocinductive{bool}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
\subsection{Instrumented semantics with branches}


\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}                         (CFE\_Skip)
                     st = \coqdocvar{skip} => st, \ensuremath{\Box}


                       aeval st a = n
\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}                 (CFE\_Asgn)
               st = \coqdocvar{x} := \coqdocvar{a} => (x !-> n ; st), \ensuremath{\Box}


      st  = \coqdocvar{c1} => st', bs1   st' = \coqdocvar{c2} => st'', bs2
\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}              (CFE\_Seq)
               st = \coqdocvar{c1};\coqdocvar{c2} => st'', (bs1++bs2)


            beval st b = true     st = \coqdocvar{c1} => st', bs1
\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}        (CFE\_IfTrue)
        st = \coqdockw{if} \coqdocvar{b} \coqdockw{then} \coqdocvar{c1} \coqdockw{else} \coqdocvar{c2} \coqdockw{end} => st', true::bs1


            beval st b = false    st = \coqdocvar{c2} => st', bs2
\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}       (CFE\_IfFalse)
       st = \coqdockw{if} \coqdocvar{b} \coqdockw{then} \coqdocvar{c1} \coqdockw{else} \coqdocvar{c2} \coqdockw{end} => st', false::bs2


  st = \coqdockw{if} \coqdocvar{b} \coqdockw{then} \coqdocvar{c}; \coqdocvar{while} \coqdocvar{b} \coqdoctac{do} \coqdocvar{c} \coqdockw{end} \coqdockw{else} \coqdocvar{skip} \coqdockw{end} => st', os
\par
\noindent\hrulefill\par
\noindent{}   (CFE\_While)
            st = \coqdocvar{while} \coqdocvar{b} \coqdoctac{do} \coqdocvar{c} \coqdockw{end} => st', os

\subsection{Control Flow security definition}



 Using the instrumented semantics we define Control Flow (CF) security: 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Definition} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.cf secure}{cf\_secure}{\coqdocdefinition{cf\_secure}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.P:283}{P}{\coqdocbinder{P}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.c:284}{c}{\coqdocbinder{c}} := \coqdockw{\ensuremath{\forall}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:285}{s1}{\coqdocbinder{s1}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:286}{s2}{\coqdocbinder{s2}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s1':287}{s1'}{\coqdocbinder{s1'}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s2':288}{s2'}{\coqdocbinder{s2'}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.bs1:289}{bs1}{\coqdocbinder{bs1}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.bs2:290}{bs2}{\coqdocbinder{bs2}},\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.P:283}{\coqdocvariable{P}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:285}{\coqdocvariable{s1}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:286}{\coqdocvariable{s2}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '->' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\rightarrow}}}\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:285}{\coqdocvariable{s1}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x ',' x}{\coqdocnotation{=[}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.c:284}{\coqdocvariable{c}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x ',' x}{\coqdocnotation{]=>}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s1':287}{\coqdocvariable{s1'}}\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x ',' x}{\coqdocnotation{,}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.bs1:289}{\coqdocvariable{bs1}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '->' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\rightarrow}}}\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:286}{\coqdocvariable{s2}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x ',' x}{\coqdocnotation{=[}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.c:284}{\coqdocvariable{c}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x ',' x}{\coqdocnotation{]=>}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s2':288}{\coqdocvariable{s2'}}\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x ',' x}{\coqdocnotation{,}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.bs2:290}{\coqdocvariable{bs2}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '->' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\rightarrow}}}\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.bs1:289}{\coqdocvariable{bs1}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '=' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{=}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.bs2:290}{\coqdocvariable{bs2}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
CF security is mostly orthogonal to noninterference and
    instead of relating the final states it requires the branches of
    the program to be independent of secrets.


    Our restrictive \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.cf well typed}{\coqdocinductive{cf\_well\_typed}} relation enforces both
    noninterference (as we already proved at the beginning of the
    chapter) and CF security: 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Theorem} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.cf well typed cf secure}{cf\_well\_typed\_cf\_secure}{\coqdoclemma{cf\_well\_typed\_cf\_secure}} : \coqdockw{\ensuremath{\forall}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.P:291}{P}{\coqdocbinder{P}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.c:292}{c}{\coqdocbinder{c}},\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.P:291}{\coqdocvariable{P}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x '|-cf-' x}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\vdash}}}\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x '|-cf-' x}{\coqdocnotation{cf}}\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x '|-cf-' x}{\coqdocnotation{-}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.c:292}{\coqdocvariable{c}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '->' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\rightarrow}}}\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.cf secure}{\coqdocdefinition{cf\_secure}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.P:291}{\coqdocvariable{P}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.c:292}{\coqdocvariable{c}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Proof}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqdoctac{intros} \coqdocvar{P} \coqdocvar{c} \coqdocvar{Hwt} \coqdocvar{s1} \coqdocvar{s2} \coqdocvar{s1'} \coqdocvar{s2'} \coqdocvar{bs1} \coqdocvar{bs2} \coqdocvar{Heq} \coqdocvar{Heval1} \coqdocvar{Heval2}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqdoctac{generalize} \coqdoctac{dependent} \coqdocvar{s2'}. \coqdoctac{generalize} \coqdoctac{dependent} \coqdocvar{s2}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqdoctac{generalize} \coqdoctac{dependent} \coqdocvar{bs2}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqdoctac{induction} \coqdocvar{Heval1}; \coqdoctac{intros} \coqdocvar{bs2'} \coqdocvar{s2} \coqdocvar{Heq} \coqdocvar{s2'} \coqdocvar{Heval2};\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{2.00em}
\coqdoctac{inversion} \coqdocvar{Heval2}; \coqdoctac{inversion} \coqdocvar{Hwt}; \coqdoctac{subst}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
- \coqdoctac{reflexivity}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
- \coqdoctac{reflexivity}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
- \coqdoctac{destruct} (\coqdocvar{IHHeval1\_1} \coqdocvar{H8} \coqdocvar{bs0} \coqdocvar{s2} \coqdocvar{Heq} \coqdocvar{st'0} \coqdocvar{H1}).\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{2.00em}
\coqdoctac{assert} (\coqdocvar{Heq'}: \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}} \coqdocvar{P} \coqdocvar{st'} \coqdocvar{st'0}).\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{2.00em}
\{ \coqdoctac{eapply} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.cf ceval ceval}{\coqdoclemma{cf\_ceval\_ceval}} \coqdoctac{in} \coqdocvar{Heval1\_1}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{3.00em}
\coqdoctac{eapply} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.cf ceval ceval}{\coqdoclemma{cf\_ceval\_ceval}} \coqdoctac{in} \coqdocvar{H1}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{3.00em}
\coqdoctac{eapply} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.cf well typed noninterferent}{\coqdoclemma{cf\_well\_typed\_noninterferent}} \coqdockw{with} (\coqdocvar{c}:=\coqdocvar{c1}); \coqdoctac{eauto}. \}\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{2.00em}
\coqdocvar{erewrite} \coqdocvar{IHHeval1\_2}; \coqdoctac{eauto}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
- \coqdoctac{f\_equal}. \coqdoctac{eapply} \coqdocvar{IHHeval1}; \coqdoctac{try} \coqdocvar{eassumption}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}

\coqdocindent{2.00em}
\coqdoctac{rewrite} \coqdocvar{H} \coqdoctac{in} \coqdocvar{H6}. \coqdoctac{discriminate} \coqdocvar{H6}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
- \coqdoctac{rewrite} (\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.noninterferent bexp}{\coqdoclemma{noninterferent\_bexp}} \coqdocvar{Heq} \coqdocvar{H11}) \coqdoctac{in} \coqdocvar{H}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{2.00em}
\coqdoctac{rewrite} \coqdocvar{H} \coqdoctac{in} \coqdocvar{H6}. \coqdoctac{discriminate} \coqdocvar{H6}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
- \coqdoctac{f\_equal}. \coqdoctac{eapply} \coqdocvar{IHHeval1}; \coqdocvar{eassumption}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}

\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Qed}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
The proof does rely on \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.cf well typed}{\coqdocinductive{cf\_well\_typed}} implying noninterference
    for the sequencing case (and indirectly for the while case too,
    since in our semantics of while evaluates to a sequence). 

 Control flow security forms the foundation on which we will define
    cryptographic constant time in the \coqdoclibrary{SpecCT} chapter. 

\paragraph{Exercise: 4 stars, standard (cf\_well\_typed\_ts\_cf\_secure)}



 We can also define a stronger, termination-sensitive version of
    control flow security: 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Definition} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.ts cf secure}{ts\_cf\_secure}{\coqdocdefinition{ts\_cf\_secure}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.P:293}{P}{\coqdocbinder{P}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.c:294}{c}{\coqdocbinder{c}} := \coqdockw{\ensuremath{\forall}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:295}{s1}{\coqdocbinder{s1}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:296}{s2}{\coqdocbinder{s2}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s1':297}{s1'}{\coqdocbinder{s1'}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.bs1:298}{bs1}{\coqdocbinder{bs1}},\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.P:293}{\coqdocvariable{P}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:295}{\coqdocvariable{s1}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:296}{\coqdocvariable{s2}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '->' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\rightarrow}}}\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:295}{\coqdocvariable{s1}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x ',' x}{\coqdocnotation{=[}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.c:294}{\coqdocvariable{c}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x ',' x}{\coqdocnotation{]=>}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s1':297}{\coqdocvariable{s1'}}\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x ',' x}{\coqdocnotation{,}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.bs1:298}{\coqdocvariable{bs1}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '->' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\rightarrow}}}\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqexternalref{::type scope:'exists' x '..' x ',' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\exists}}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s2':299}{s2'}{\coqdocbinder{s2'}}\coqexternalref{::type scope:'exists' x '..' x ',' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{,}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:296}{\coqdocvariable{s2}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x ',' x}{\coqdocnotation{=[}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.c:294}{\coqdocvariable{c}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x ',' x}{\coqdocnotation{]=>}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s2':299}{\coqdocvariable{s2'}}\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x ',' x}{\coqdocnotation{,}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.bs1:298}{\coqdocvariable{bs1}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
In this exercise, you have to prove that \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.cf well typed}{\coqdocinductive{cf\_well\_typed}} also
    implies \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.ts cf secure}{\coqdocdefinition{ts\_cf\_secure}}. The while case should actually be quite
    easy, if you exploit how we reduced evaluation of while to
    sequencing and \coqdockw{if}-\coqdockw{then}-\coqdockw{else} in rule \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.CFE While}{\coqdocconstructor{CFE\_While}} above.

\section{Exercise: Adding public outputs}



\paragraph{Exercise: 5 stars, standard (public\_outputs)}



 Imp, the simple imperative language we considered so far, doesn't
    have an output operation. In practice, however, programs often
    need to produce publicly-observable outputs. In this exercise, we
    extend our language with an output command and introduce an
    additional security property to be enforced for such programs.

We modify the command evaluation to explicitly track outputs.
    Instead of the previous evaluation relation \coqdocvar{st} =[ \coqdocvar{c} ]=> \coqdocvar{st'}, we
    now use the \coqdocvar{st} =[ \coqdocvar{c} ]=> \coqdocvar{st'}, \coqdocvar{os} relation below, where \coqdocvar{os}
    represents the sequence of outputs produced during evaluation.

The original noninterference definition, which only compares final
    states, does not guarantee security of the publicly-observable outputs.


    Although \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.OUTPUT.output insecure com1}{\coqdocdefinition{output\_insecure\_com1}} and \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.OUTPUT.output insecure com2}{\coqdocdefinition{output\_insecure\_com2}} below obviously leak
    secret through their outputs they still satisfy noninterference.

We define an output security property inspired by control flow
    security. Instead of relating final states like noninterference,
    we require that a program's outputs be independent of secrets. 
\begin{coqdoccode}
\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Definition} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.OUTPUT.output secure}{output\_secure}{\coqdocdefinition{output\_secure}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.P:351}{P}{\coqdocbinder{P}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.c:352}{c}{\coqdocbinder{c}} := \coqdockw{\ensuremath{\forall}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:353}{s1}{\coqdocbinder{s1}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:354}{s2}{\coqdocbinder{s2}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s1':355}{s1'}{\coqdocbinder{s1'}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.o1:356}{o1}{\coqdocbinder{o1}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.s2':357}{s2'}{\coqdocbinder{s2'}} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.o2:358}{o2}{\coqdocbinder{o2}},\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.P:351}{\coqdocvariable{P}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:353}{\coqdocvariable{s1}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:354}{\coqdocvariable{s2}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '->' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\rightarrow}}}\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s1:353}{\coqdocvariable{s1}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.OUTPUT.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x ',' x}{\coqdocnotation{=[}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.c:352}{\coqdocvariable{c}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.OUTPUT.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x ',' x}{\coqdocnotation{]=>}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s1':355}{\coqdocvariable{s1'}}\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.OUTPUT.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x ',' x}{\coqdocnotation{,}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.o1:356}{\coqdocvariable{o1}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '->' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\rightarrow}}}\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s2:354}{\coqdocvariable{s2}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.OUTPUT.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x ',' x}{\coqdocnotation{=[}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.c:352}{\coqdocvariable{c}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.OUTPUT.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x ',' x}{\coqdocnotation{]=>}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.s2':357}{\coqdocvariable{s2'}}\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.OUTPUT.:::x '=[' x ']=>' x ',' x}{\coqdocnotation{,}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.o2:358}{\coqdocvariable{o2}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '->' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{\ensuremath{\rightarrow}}}\coqdoceol
\coqdocindent{1.00em}
\coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.o1:356}{\coqdocvariable{o1}} \coqexternalref{::type scope:x '=' x}{http://rocq-prover.org/doc/V9.1.1/stdlib//Corelib.Init.Logic}{\coqdocnotation{=}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.o2:358}{\coqdocvariable{o2}}.\coqdoceol
\coqdocemptyline
\end{coqdoccode}
This property disallows programs whose outputs depend on secrets:

\coqdocemptyline
\coqdocnoindent
\coqdockw{Lemma} \coqdef{SECF.StaticIFC.OUTPUT.output insecure output insecure com1}{output\_insecure\_output\_insecure\_com1}{\coqdoclemma{output\_insecure\_output\_insecure\_com1}} :\coqdoceol

\coqdoctac{assert} (\coqdocvar{PEQUIV}: \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.pub equiv}{\coqdocdefinition{pub\_equiv}} \coqref{SECF.StaticIFC.xpub}{\coqdocdefinition{xpub}} \coqdocvar{s1} \coqdocvar{s2}).\coqdoceol

In the following tasks, you will define a type system enforcing
    both noninterference and output security. Then, you will write a
    type-checker and prove that it is sound and complete with respect
    to the type system. Finally, you will prove that your type system
    implies both noninterference and output security.


    All lemmas and theorems marked as \coqdocvar{Admitted} provide partial
    credit, even if you cannot prove everything.
